<user>
I have a question about reporting suspected wrongdoing at CSULB. What does the policy say?
</user>
<bot>
According to the Policy on Fraud, section "Reporting Suspected Wrongdoing": Any irregularity that is detected or suspected must be reported immediately to the Director of Administrative Services and/or by contacting The Network fraud hotline at 888-823-6298 or on-line at www.reportlineweb.com/csulb. The individual filing the report may remain anonymous. Any representative who suspects dishonest or fraudulent activity must not attempt personally to conduct investigations, interviews, or interrogations related to any suspected fraudulent act.
Source: https://asicsulb.org/corporate/documents/discover/policies/policy-on-fraud.pdf
</bot>

</user>
<bot>
According to the Time, Place and Manner, section "Electronic Marquees": CSULB operates the 7th Street Marquee that may be used by campus-affiliated organizations and departments to advertise campus events. To request permission to use the 7th Street Marquee, located at the corner of 7th Street and West Campus Drive, complete the Marquee Submissions form. Requests should be submitted at least three weeks before the event date and will only be considered upon event management compliance with the CSULB Administrative Policy for Facilities Use and Procedural Guidelines.
Source: https://www.csulb.edu/university-policies/time-place-and-manner

</user>
<bot>
According to CSULB Policy Statement 23-24 (Repetition of Courses), section 5.5.2 (Early Promotion): To receive a favorable recommendation for early promotion to associate professor or full professor, a candidate must achieve a record of distinction in all three areas that clearly exceeds in substantial ways the requirements in department policies. Colleges and Departments must make clear what qualifies as exceeding in substantial ways.

What does the CSULB Regulation VIII: Recognition And Registration For Student Organizations say about hazing?
</user>
<bot>
According to the Regulation VIII: Recognition And Registration For Student Organizations, section "Hazing": Any method or act of initiation or preinitiation into (or the maintenance of status within) a student organization or student body, whether or not the organization or body is officially recognized by the CSU, which is likely to cause serious bodily injury or mental harm to any former, current, or prospective student of the CSU is prohibited and constitutes a violation of this policy.
Source: https://www.csulb.edu/student-affairs/campus-rules-and-regulations/regulation-viii

<user>
Tell me about referendum petition in the ASI Bylaws 2025-26.
</user>
<bot>
According to the ASI Bylaws 2025-26, section "Referendum Petition": Any legislation passed by the Senate, except emergency or financial measures, may be subject to the referendum vote of the members of the Associated Students. Upon presentation to the President of a written petition signed by 5% of the regular members of the Associated Students, the Senate will repeal such legislation or submit it to a vote of the members of the Associated Students.
Source: https://asicsulb.org/corporate/documents/gov/Bylaws%202025-2026-v2.pdf
</bot>

<user>
Give me an overview of the CSULB General Education Evaluation Committee (GEEC).
</user>
<bot>

What is the CSULB policy on definitions?
</user>

What does the CSULB Jeanne Clery Campus Safety Act say about working days from end of hearing?
</user>
<bot>
According to the Jeanne Clery Campus Safety Act, section 15 (Working Days from end of hearing): Opening Statements Each Party will be given an opportunity to make an opening statement of no longer than 10 minutes. An opening statement is intended to give the Parties the opportunity to share their perspective regarding the facts and discuss the core disputes in the investigation. It should focus on the facts of the matter and not be argumentative.
Source: https://www.csulb.edu/sites/default/files/2026/documents/OEC-2026_Clery_ASR.pdf

When initiating procurement of personal services, the proper type of contract agreement must be selected, appropriate clauses must be included, and appropriate controls instituted over the contracted effort.

What is the CSULB policy on criteria for area 4C, social and behavioral sciences and history?
</user>
<bot>
According to CSULB Policy Statement 24-20 (Policy on General Education), section 3.2.4.2.4 (Criteria for Area 4C, Social and Behavioral Sciences and History): Courses in fulfillment of Area 4C will foster in students an awareness that human social, political, and economic institutions and behavior are inextricably interwoven. Problems and issues in these areas may be examined in their contemporary as well as historical settings.
Source: https://www.csulb.edu/academic-senate/policy-general-education-0
</bot>

<user>
Summarize the Upper-Division Courses in the Baccalaureate Major, Ten-Year Rule.
</user>
<bot>
According to CSULB Policy Statement 85-15 (Upper-Division Courses in the Baccalaureate Major, Ten-Year Rule): California State University, Long Beach Policy Statement 85-15 To assure that a student's preparation in the major is coherent and current, all upperdivision courses required for a major must be completed within the ten-year period preceding award of the baccalaureate degree. Courses completed prior to this ten-year period can be revalidated by such demonstrations of competence or knowledge of the subject as may be prescribed by the department offering the course.
Source: https://web.csulb.edu/divisions/academic_affairs/grad_undergrad/senate/policy/academic/alphabetical/documents/85-15TenYearRuleUpperDivisonCoursesintheBaccalaureateMajor.pdf
</bot>

<user>
What does the CSULB Policy on Information Systems say about suspicious personal identifying information?
</user>
<bot>
According to the Policy on Information Systems, section "Suspicious Personal Identifying Information": The address or telephone number provided is the same as or similar to the address number or telephone number submitted by an unusually large number of other persons opening accounts or other customers. The person opening the covered account or the customer fails to provide all required personal identifying information on an application or in response to notification that the application is incomplete.
Source: https://asicsulb.org/corporate/documents/discover/policies/policy-on-information-systems.pdf
</bot>

Summarize the Policies for CSULB Extension Programs.
</user>
<bot>
According to the Policies for CSULB Extension Programs: Approved 5/15/70 Resident and non-resident faculty appointments to extension courses shall be recommended by the Department Chairman, approved by the Dean of the School, and forwarded to the Dean or Director of Extension for implementation. Non-resident appointments shall not exceed the level of rank that would be offered the same individual for a full-time resident appointment.
Source: https://www.csulb.edu/academic-senate/policies-for-csulb-extension-programs

Tell me about peer review in the 19-06 Range Elevation for Lecturer Faculty.
</user>
<bot>
According to CSULB Policy Statement 19-06 (19-06 Range Elevation for Lecturer Faculty), section 7.0 (Peer Review): Denial of range elevation shall be subject to a peer review process. Each year a Faculty Hearing Committee established in accordance with Article 10.12 of the CBA shall serve to hear appeals of any temporary faculty unit employee denied range elevation during that fiscal year. The Committee shall allow for appellants to make a presentation to the Committee and to be represented by CFA if so desired.
Source: https://www.csulb.edu/academic-senate/19-06-range-elevation-for-lecturer-faculty

Hazardous Substances Training Guide - what are the rules at CSULB?
</user>
<bot>
According to the Policy on Hazard Communication Program, section "Hazardous Substances Training Guide": Medical experts have reviewed the data on many hazardous substances to determine how toxic they are and to set permissible exposure levels (OSHA standards) called PELs to limit the dose that workers receive of these substances. These standards are included on M/SDS and are used to evaluate workplace exposure monitoring results.
Source: https://asicsulb.org/corporate/documents/discover/policies/policy-on-hazard-communication-program.pdf
